BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                        業務回顧
On behalf of the board of directors (the “Board”), I am delighted to report to         本人謹代表董事會（「董事會」），欣然向各位股
our shareholders that substantial revenue and earnings growth was recorded             東公佈，建滔積層板控股有限公司及其附屬公
in the financial year ended 31 December 2021 for Kingboard Laminates                   司（「集團」）截至二零二一年十二月三十一日止
Holdings Limited and its subsidiaries (the “Group”).                                   財政年度（「回顧期」）之全年業績，營業額及利
                                                                                       潤均錄得大幅增長。

2021 witnessed strong retail market demands as global economies continued              於二零二一年，全球經濟持續復甦，零售市場
to be recovered. The surge in demand for consumer electronics drove strong             需求強勁，各類電子消費產品需求激增，覆銅
growth in the laminates market. The explosive growth of new energy vehicles,           面板市場呈現快速增長。新能源汽車領域呈現
coupled with intelligent upgrades of automobile systems, has stimulated                爆發式增長，再加上汽車系統智能化升級，刺
vehicle sales growth. Sales of home appliances grew significantly, benefitting         激汽車銷量進一步提升。家電產品受惠於居家
from increased time spent at home, while also being driven by the introduction         時間增多，家電產品多樣化及高端化，推陳出
of newer and high-end models. Sales of computers and servers continued to              新以配合消費者需求，家電產品銷售顯著上
boom, driven by remote work, online education and web conferencing needs.              升。電腦和伺服器在遠程辦公、在線教育及線
The market for downstream electronic products has set off a consumption                上會議應用提升的帶動下，銷情持續暢旺。下
boom, and the Group has seized this opportunity to achieve substantial                 游電子產品市場掀起消費熱潮，集團把握市場
increase in laminates sales. Supply of laminates and their upstream materials          良機，推動覆銅面板銷售量大幅提升。在需求
tightened as a result of demand growth, driving price increases. The Group is          拉動下，覆銅面板及其上游物料的供應也轉趨
equipped with a comprehensive upstream value chain that boasts high quality            緊張，產品售價亦乘勢而上。集團擁有配套完
and advanced technology. This value chain enables the Group to integrate               善、品質上乘及技術領先的銅箔、玻璃絲、玻
copper foil, glass yarn, glass fabric and epoxy resins into its capability, which      璃布和環氧樹脂之上游物料產業鏈，能為覆銅

advantage fully utilised, the profit margin for laminates recorded a significant       優勢得以充分發揮，覆銅面板利潤率因而錄得
increase.                                                                              顯著增幅。

PERMITTED INDEMNITY PROVISION                                                       獲准許的彌償條文
According to the articles of association of the Company, every Director,            根據本公司組織章程細則，本公司各董事、核
auditor or other officer of the Company shall be entitled to be indemnified         數師或其他高級人員就其作為本公司董事、核
out of the assets of the Company against all losses or liabilities incurred or      數師或其他高級人員進行民事或刑事抗辯（獲判
sustained by him as a Director, auditor or other officer of the Company in          勝訴或無罪）而產生或蒙受之損失或負債，均可
defending any proceedings, whether civil or criminal, in which judgment is          從本公司之資產中獲得彌償。
given in his favour, or in which he is acquitted.

liabilities arising out of activities of the Group has been arranged by the         層因本集團活動引起的責任。
Company.

operate in a transparent manner with the adoptions of various self-regulatory      並清楚界定董事與管理層權責，乃符合權益持
policies, procedures and monitoring mechanisms and a clear definition of           有人及股東之利益。
accountability of directors and management.

STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the                我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存
consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material                在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得
misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s report      合理保證，並按照我們商定的業務約定條款僅
that includes our opinion solely to you, as a body, in accordance with our         向全體股東出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。
agreed terms of engagement, and for no other purpose. We do not assume             除此以外，我們的報告不可用作其他用途。我
responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents    們並不就本報告之內容對任何其他人士承擔任
of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not      何責任或接受任何義務。合理保證是高水準的
a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will always           保證，但不能保證按照《香港審核準則》進行的
detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from        審核，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。
fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate,   錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預
they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of           期它們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表
users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，
                                                                                   則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。

As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise professional             在根據《香港審核準則》進行審核的過程中，我
judgement and maintain professional skepticism throughout the audit. We            們運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。我
also:                                                                              們亦：

•      Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the               •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合
       consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,               財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設
       design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks,                  計及執行審核程序以應對這些風險，以
       and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate to                 及獲取充足和適當的審核憑證，作為我
       provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material           們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串
       misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from         謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或淩
       error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional omissions,          駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺
       misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.                        詐而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未
                                                                                       能發現因錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述的
                                                                                       風險。

•      Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit           •   瞭解與審核相關的內部控制，以設計適

       the effectiveness of the Group’s internal control.

       made by the directors.

     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKFRS 9, HKAS 39,                       香港財務申報準則第9號、香港會計準
     HKFRS 7, HKFRS 4 and HKFRS 16 “Interest Rate Benchmark                          則第39號、香港財務申報準則第7號、

                                                                                     革－第二階段」之影響
     The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the                  本集團於本年度首次應用該等修訂本。
     current year. The amendments relate to changes in the basis for                 該修訂涉及因利率基準改革而改變釐定
     determining the contractual cash flows of financial assets, financial           金融資產、金融負債和租賃合約之現金
     liabilities and lease liabilities as a result of interest rate benchmark        流量基準、特定對沖會計規定及相關應
     reform, specific hedge accounting requirements and the related                  用香港財務申報準則第7號「金融工具：
     disclosure requirements applying HKFRS 7 “Financial Instruments:                披露」（「香港財務申報準則第7號」）的披
     Disclosures” (“HKFRS 7”).                                                       露規定。

     As at 1 January 2021, the Group has several financial liabilities, the          於二零二一年一月一日，本集團擁有若
     interests of which are indexed to benchmark rates that will or may be           干金融負債，其利息與將或可能進行利
     subject to interest rate benchmark reform. The following table shows            率基準改革的基準利率掛鉤。下表載列
     the total amounts of these outstanding contracts. The amounts of                了該等未償還合約之總額。該等金融負
     financial liabilities are shown at their carrying amounts are shown at          債金額按賬面值列賬。
     their notional amounts.

                                                                                                 Hong Kong Dollars
                                                                                                       Hong Kong
                                                                                                          Interbank

2.   APPLICATION OF AMENDMENTS TO                                                 2.   應用經修訂之香港財務
     HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING                                                     申報準則（「香港財務申
     STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                                  報準則」）
                                                                                           （續）

     Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily effective                               於本年度已強制生效之經修訂之
     for the current year (continued)                                                  香港財務申報準則（續）
     Impacts on application of the agenda decision of the                              應用委員會─出售存貨所需之成本之議
     Committee – Costs necessary to sell inventories (HKAS 2                           程決定（香港會計準則第2號「存貨」
                                                                                                       （「香
     “Inventories” (“HKAS 2”))                                                         港會計準則第2號」））之影響
     In June 2021, the Committee, through its agenda decision, clarified               於二零二一年六月，委員會通過其議程
     the costs an entity should include as “estimated costs necessary                  決定釐清實體在釐定存貨之可變現淨值
     to make the sale” when determining the net realisable value of                    時之成本應列為「進行銷售所需之估計
     inventories. In particular, whether such costs should be limited to               成本」。尤其是，有關成本是否應僅限
     those that are incremental to the sale. The Committee concluded that              於銷售增量成本。委員會認為，進行
     the estimated costs necessary to make the sale should not be limited              銷售所需之估計成本不應僅限於增量成
     to those that are incremental but should also include costs that an               本，亦應包括實體出售其存貨須承擔之
     entity must incur to sell its inventories including those that are not            成本，包括非特定銷售增量成本。
     incremental to a particular sale.

     The Group’s accounting policy prior to the Committee’s agenda                     本集團於委員會議程決定前之會計政策
     decision was to determine the net realisable value of inventories                 為僅計及增量成本釐定可變現淨值。於
     taking into consideration incremental costs only. Upon application of             應用委員會議程決定後，本集團改變其
     the Committee’s agenda decision, the Group changed its accounting                 會計政策，在釐定存貨之可變現淨值時
     policy to determine the net realisable value of inventories taking into           計及增量成本及出售存貨所需的其他成
     consideration both incremental costs and other costs necessary                    本。新會計政策已獲追溯應用。
     to sell inventories. The new accounting policy has been applied
     retrospectively.

     The application of the Committee’s agenda decision has had no                     應用委員會議程決定對本集團財務狀況
     material impact on the Group’s financial positions and performance.               及表現並無重大影響。

           statements include applicable disclosures required by the                    表載有聯交所證券上市規則 （「上

           Ordinance.

           Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset                   公平值是於計量日期市場參與者
           or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between                 間於有秩序交易中出售資產所收
           market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                        取或轉讓負債須支付之價格，而
           whether that price is directly observable or estimated using                      不論該價格為可直接觀察取得或
           another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value                         可使用其他估值方法估計。於估
           of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the                      計資產或負債之公平值時，本集
           characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants                  團會考慮該等市場參與者於計量
           would take those characteristics into account when pricing                        日期對資產或負債定價時所考慮
           the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value                        之資產或負債之特點。於該等綜
           for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these                               合財務報表中作計量及╱或披露
           consolidated financial statements is determined on such a                         用途之公平值乃按此基準釐定，
           basis, except for share-based payment transactions that                           惟以下各項除外：屬於香港財
           are within the scope of HKFRS 2 “Share-based Payment”,                            務申報準則第2號「以股份形式
           leasing transactions that are accounted for in accordance with                    付款」範圍內之以股份形式付款
           HKFRS 16 “Leases” (“HKFRS 16”), and measurements that                             交易、按照香港財務申報準則第
           have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such                 16號「租賃」
                                                                                                   （「香港財務申報準
           as net realisable value in HKAS 2 or value in use in HKAS 36                      則第16號」）入賬之租賃交易，
           “Impairment of Assets”.                                                           以及與公平值存在某些相似之處
                                                                                             但並非公平值之項目計量，例如
                                                                                             香港會計準則第2號之可變現淨
                                                                                             值或香港會計準則第36號「資產
                                                                                             減值」之使用價值。

           A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                      非金融資產公平值之計量則參考
           account a market participant’s ability to generate economic                       市場參與者可從使用該資產得到
           benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by                     之最高及最佳效用，或把該資產
           selling it to another market participant that would use the asset                 售予另一可從使用該資產得到最
           in its highest and best use.                                                      高及最佳效用之市場參與者所產
                                                                                             生之經濟效益。

                                                                                             損益表，直至本集團對該附屬公

           transfer of the associated goods and services are accounted                     支，使用與借貸成本相同的基準

           Leases                                                                          租賃
           Definition of a lease                                                           租賃的定義
           A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the                 倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段
           right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of                 時間內控制使用可識別資產的權
           time in exchange for consideration.                                             利，則該合約屬租賃或包含租
                                                                                           賃。

           For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date                     就於首次應用香港財務申報準則
           of initial application of HKFRS 16 or arising from business                     第16號日期或之後訂立或修改
           combinations, the Group assesses whether a contract is                          或源於業務合併的合約而言，本
           or contains a lease based on the definition under HKFRS                         集團根據香港財務申報準則第
           16 at inception, modification date or acquisition date, as                      16號的定義於初始或修改或收
           appropriate. Such contract will not be reassessed unless                        購日期（視乎情況而定）評估該合
           the terms and conditions of the contract are subsequently                       約是否為租賃或包含租賃。除非
           changed.                                                                        合約的條款及條件其後出現變
                                                                                           動，否則有關合約將不予重新評
                                                                                           估。

           The Group as a lessee                                                           本集團作為承租人
           Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                         將代價分配至合約組成部分
           For a contract that contains a lease component and one or                       就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或
           more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group                        多項額外租賃或非租賃組成部分
           allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease                       的合約而言，本集團根據租賃組
           component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of                     成部分的相對獨立價格及非租賃
           the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price of                      組成部分的合計獨立價格基準將
           the non-lease components, including contract for acquisition                    合約代價分配至各項租賃組成部
           of ownership interests of a property which includes both                        分，包括收購含有租賃土地及非
           leasehold land and non-lease building components, unless                        租賃樓宇組成部分的物業的所有
           such allocation cannot be made reliably.                                        權權益的合約，惟有關分配無法
                                                                                           可靠作出則除外。

           The Group applies practical expedient not to separate non-                      本集團應用可行權宜之計不將非
           lease components from lease component, and instead                              租賃組成部分與租賃組成部分分
           account for the lease component and any associated non-                         開，而將租賃組成部分及任何相
           lease components as a single lease component.                                   關非租賃組成部分作為單一租賃
                                                                                           組成部分入賬。

                  liability;                                                                 額；

                  received;                                                                  何已收租賃優惠；

           •      any initial direct costs incurred by the Group; and                    •   本集團產生的任何初始
                                                                                             直接成本；及

           lease liabilities.                                                            重新計量時作出調整。

            Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as                          本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為
            finance or operating leases. Whenever the terms of the lease                      融資或經營租賃。倘租賃條款將
            transfer substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to                    有關資產擁有權附帶的絕大部分

            classified as a finance lease. All other leases are classified as                 分類為融資租賃。所有其他租賃
            operating leases.                                                                 均分類為經營租賃。

            Rental income from operating leases is recognised in profit                       經營租賃的租金收益乃按相關租

            lease agreement. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating                     認。磋商及安排經營租賃過程中
            and arranging an operating lease are added to the carrying                        產生的初始直接成本計入租賃資
            amount of the leased asset, and such costs are recognised as                      產的賬面值，有關成本於租期內
            an expense on a straight-line basis over the lease term except                    按直線法確認為開支，按公平值
            for investment properties measured under fair value model.                        模式計量的投資物業除外。

            Rental income which is derived from the Group’s ordinary                          本集團日常業務過程中產生的租
            course of business are presented as revenue.                                      賃收入呈列為營業額。

            Lease modification                                                                租賃的修改
            Changes in considerations of lease contracts that were not                        不屬於原有條款及條件的租賃合
            part of the original terms and conditions are accounted for                       約考慮因素的變化作為租賃修改
            as lease modifications, including lease incentives provided                       入賬，包括通過免除或減少租金
            through forgiveness or reduction of rentals.                                      提供的租賃激勵。

            The Group accounts for a modification to an operating lease                       本集團自修改生效日期起將經營

            lease.                                                                            一部分。

            with a corresponding adjustment to share-based payments                             計，以股份形式付款儲備亦作相
            reserve. For share options that vest immediately at the date of                     應之調整。就於授出日期即時歸
            grant, the fair value of the share options granted is expensed                      屬的購股權而言，已獲授購股權
            immediately to profit or loss.                                                      的公平值即時於損益支銷。

            When the share options are exercised, the amount previously                         購股權獲行使時，過往於以股份
            recognised in the share-based payments reserve will be                              形式付款儲備中確認之數額將轉
            transferred to share premium. When the share options are                            移至股份溢價。當購股權於歸屬
            forfeited after vesting date or are still not exercised at the                      日後被沒收或於屆滿日仍未獲行
            expiry date, the amount previously recognised in share                              使，則過往於購股權儲備中確認
            options reserve will be transferred to retained profits.                            之金額將轉撥至保留溢利。

            appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on                   價）精確折現至初步確認時之賬
            initial recognition.                                                           面淨值之比率。

            represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to                      個月預期信貸虧損指預計因報告

            that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions                   往信用損失經驗作出，並就債務

            (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                              (iii)   信貸減值金融資產
                    A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more                                 當發生一項或多項對金
                    events that have a detrimental impact on the estimated                                融資產估計未來現金流
                    future cash flows of that financial asset have occurred.                              量有不利影響之事件
                    Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired                                    時，金融資產出現信貸

                                                                                                                難；

                    (b)     a breach of contract, such as a default or past                               (b)   違反合約，如違
                            due event;                                                                          約或逾期事件；

                    (c)     the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                                (c)   借款人之放款人
                            contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                                      因與借款人有關
                            financial difficulty, having granted to the                                         之經濟或合約原

4.    CRITICAL ACCOUNTING                                                         4.   重大會計判斷及估計不
      JUDGEMENTS AND KEY SOURCES OF                                                    明朗因素之主要來源
      ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY
      In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are                 在應用本集團之會計政策（於附註3載
      described in Note 3, the Directors are required to make judgements,              述）過程中，董事須就未能即時自其他
      estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts of assets                   來源取得資產及負債賬面值，作出判
      and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.                斷、估計及假設。該等估計及相關假設
      The estimates and underlying assumptions are based on historical                 按照過往經驗及其他被視為相關的其他
      experience and other factors that are considered to be relevant.                 因素釐定。實際結果可能有別於該等估
      Actual results may differ from these estimates.                                  計。

      The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-                  估計及相關假設會持續審閱。如會計估
      going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in the             計的修改僅影響修改的期間，則在該期
      period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only that        間確認。如修改影響現時及未來期間，
      period, or in the period of the revision and future periods if revision          則在修改期間及未來期間均會確認。
      affects both current and future periods.


      Critical judgements in applying accounting policies                              應用會計政策之重大判斷
      The following are the critical judgements, apart from those involving            除涉及估計（見下文）外，以下為董事應
      estimations (see below), that the Directors have made in the process             用本集團會計政策過程中作出，並對綜
      of applying the Group’s accounting policies and that have the most               合財務報表內確認之款額造成最重大影
      significant effect on the amounts recognised in consolidated financial           響的重大判斷。
      statements.

      Deferred taxation on investment properties (Notes 14 and 19)                     投資物業之遞延稅項（附註14及19）
      For the purposes of measuring deferred tax liabilities or deferred tax           就計量利用公平值模式的投資物業所產
      assets arising from investment properties that are measured using the            生的遞延稅項負債或遞延稅項資產而
      fair value model, the Directors have reviewed the Group’s investment             言，董事已檢討本集團於二零二一年

      The revenue of the Group arising from sales of glass epoxy laminates,        本集團自銷售環氧玻璃纖維覆銅面板、

      at a point in time. Under the transfer-of-control approach in                生的營業額乃於某一時間點確認。根據
      HKFRS 15, revenue from these sales is recognised when customer               香港財務申報準則第15號的控制權轉移
      acceptance has been obtained, which is the point of time when                法，該等銷售收益乃於獲得客戶接受時
      the customer has the ability to direct the use of these products and         確認，即當客戶有能力主導該等產品的
      obtain substantially all of the remaining benefits of these products.        使用及獲得該等產品絕大部分餘下利益
                                                                                   的時間點。

      The revenue of the Group arising from sales of properties in the             本集團於日常業務過程中銷售物業產生
      ordinary course of business is recognised at a point in time when the        的營業額於客戶獲得有關物業的控制權
      customer obtains control of the respective properties.                       之時間點予以確認。

      Revenue from hotel accommodation is recognised over time, as                 酒店住宿營業額隨時間確認，因為客戶
      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the benefits               隨本集團履約同時收取及耗用由本集團
      provided by the Group’s performance as the Group performs.                   履約所帶來的利益。

      For revenue from service income from drilling services is recognised         對於服務類營業額，鑽孔服務的收益隨
      over time, as the entity’s performance creates or enhances an asset          時間確認，原因是本集團履約創建或提
      that the customer controls as the asset is created or enhanced.              升一項於創建或提升時由客戶控制的資
                                                                                   產。

6.    SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                             6.   分部資料
      HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” (“HKFRS 8”) requires operating                           香 港 財 務 申 報 準 則 第 8 號「 經 營 分 部 」
      segments to be identified on the basis of internal reports about                     （「香港財務申報準則第8號」）要求以主
      components of the Group that are regularly reviewed by Chief                          要營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」）在對
      Operating Decision Maker (“CODM”) in order to allocate resources to                   分部作資源分配及評估其表現上所定期
      segments and to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s                   審閱的有關集團不同部門之內部報告作
      reportable segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into three main                       為確定經營分部之基準。具體而言，於
      operating divisions – (i) manufacturing and sale of laminates; (ii)                   香港財務申報準則第8號下，本集團之
      properties; and (iii) investments.                                                    須予呈報之分部分為三個主要經營分
                                                                                            部－(i)製造及銷售覆銅面板；(ii)物業；
                                                                                            及(iii)投資。

      The accounting policies the Group used for segment reporting under                   本集團根據香港財務申報準則第8號用
      HKFRS 8 are the same as those used in its HKFRS consolidated                         作分部呈報之會計政策，與其用於香港
      financial statements. Segment profit represents the profit earned by                 財務申報準則之綜合財務報表一致。分
      each segment with certain items not included (unallocated corporate                  部溢利指除若干項目（未分配企業收入
      income and expenses and finance costs). This is the measure                          及開支及財務成本）外，各分部所賺取
      reported to the CODM for the purpose of resource allocation and                      的溢利。這是報告給主要營運決策者用
      performance assessment.                                                              於資源分配和績效評估的措施。

      been levied at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% on the                      據物業銷售收入扣去指定直接成本）以

      the properties, followed by final ascertainment of the gain at the

      The Inland Revenue (Amendment) (No. 7) Bill 2017 (the “Bill”) which                  會通過了「二零一七年稅務（修訂）  （ 第7

      Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime, the first HK$2 million of             一八年三月二十八日簽署成為法律，

      above HK$2 million will be taxed at 16.5%. The profits of group                      制，合資格集團實體的首二百萬港元利

      rentals payable monthly. The leases typically run for a fixed period of           每月收取租金。租賃一般附有介乎1至
      1 to 17 years (2020: 1 to 17 years).                                              17年的固定租期（二零二零年：1至17
                                                                                        年）。

      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of the              本集團不會因租賃安排而承擔外匯風
      lease arrangement, as all leases are denominated in the respective                險，因為所有租賃均以集團實體各自的
      functional currencies of group entities. The lease contracts do not               功能貨幣列值。租約並不包括餘值擔保
      contain residual value guarantee and/or lessee’s option to purchase               及╱或承租人於租期末購買物業的選擇
      the property at the end of lease term.                                            權。

      The fair value of the Group’s investment properties at 31 December                本集團投資物業於二零二一年及二零二
      2021 and 2020 had been arrived at on the basis of a valuation carried             零年十二月三十一日之公平值乃按與本
      out by Ravia Global Appraisal Advisory Limited, an independent                    集團並無關連之獨立合資格估值師瑞豐
      qualified valuer not connected to the Group. Ravia Global Appraisal               環球評估諮詢有限公司進行估值之基準
      Advisory Limited is a member of the Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors.             達致。瑞豐環球評估諮詢有限公司為香
      The valuation was determined based on direct comparison method by                 港測量師學會會員。估值乃按直接比較
      reference to the market observable transactions of similar properties             法參考類似物業之可觀察市場交易並作
      and adjusted to reflect conditions and locations of subject properties.           出調整以反映目標物業之狀況及位置後
      There has been no change from the valuation technique used in prior               釐定。於過往年度所用之估值技術並無
      years.                                                                            變動。

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the                     為釐定相關物業的公平值，管理層就公
      management determines the appropriate valuation techniques and                    平值計量釐定適當的估值技術及輸入數
      inputs for fair value measurements.                                               據。

      The Group engages an independent valuer to perform its valuations.                本集團委聘獨立估值師估值。管理層與
      The management works closely with the independent valuer                          獨立估值師緊密合作，共同制定模型之
      to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and inputs to                   適當估值技術及輸入數據。財務事宜負
      the model. The persons in charge of financial matters report the                  責人每季向董事匯報管理層之發現，闡
      management findings to the Directors every quarter to explain the                 明資產及負債公平值波動之因由。
      cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets and liabilities.

      Under the EIT Law of the PRC, withholding tax is imposed on                       根據中國企業所得稅法，自二零零八年
      dividends declared in respect of profits earned by PRC subsidiaries               一月一日起，有關中國附屬公司所賺取
      from 1 January 2008 onwards. Deferred taxation has not been                       盈利宣派之股息須繳納預扣稅。由於本
      provided for in the consolidated financial statements in respect of               集團可控制撥回暫時性差額之時間，而
      temporary differences attributable to accumulated profits of the PRC              有關暫時性差額於可預見之將來不會撥
      subsidiaries as the Group is able to control the timing of the reversal           回，故此並無於綜合財務報表內就中國
      of the temporary differences and it is probable that the temporary                附屬公司之累計溢利所應佔之暫時性差
      differences will not reverse in the foreseeable future.                           額有關之遞延稅項作出撥備。

32. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                        32. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will           資本風險管理本集團管理其資本以確保
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to             通過優化債務與股本結存為權益持有人
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.             爭取最高回報，使本集團旗下實體能夠
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.                   持續經營。本集團之整體策略與過往年
                                                                                        度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings                    誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity                資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債（扣
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share                    除現金及現金等價物）及本公司持有人
      capital, other reserves and retained profits as disclosed in the                  應佔權益（包括已發行股本、其他儲備
      consolidated statement of changes in equity.                                      及保留溢利）。

      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.                董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and            部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the            別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through           將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as                 份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.                 衡整體資本結構。

            Credit risk and impairment assessment                                         信貸風險及減值評估
            Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s counterparties                信貸風險指本集團交易對手因違
            default on their contractual obligations resulting in financial               反其合約義務而導致本集團產生
            losses to the Group. The Group’s maximum exposure                             財務虧損的風險。倘交易方於報
            to credit risk in the event of the counterparties’ failure to                 告期末未能履行彼等就各類已確
            perform their obligations at the end of the reporting period                  認金融資產之承擔，則本集團須
            in relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the               承受之最高信貸風險為於綜合財
            carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated                 務狀況表所載該等資產賬面值。
            statement of financial position.

            Trade receivables arising from contracts with customers                       由客戶合約產生的貿易應收賬款
            In order to minimise the credit risk, the Directors has delegated             由客戶合約產生的貿易應收賬款
            the management to be responsible for determination of credit                  為將信貸風險降至最低，董事已
            limits, credit approvals for determination of credit limits, credit           委派管理層負責制訂信貸限額、
            approvals and other for monitoring procedures to ensure that                  為釐定信貸限額進行信貸審批、
            follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts. In this                   信貸審批及其他監控程序，以確
            regard, the Directors consider that the Group’s credit risk is                保採取跟進措施收回逾期未付之
            significantly reduced.                                                        債項。就此而言，本公司董事認
                                                                                          為，本集團之信貸風險已大幅降
                                                                                          低。

            The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk                     本集團並無高度集中之貿易應收
            on trade receivables with exposure spread over a number                       賬款信貸風險，風險由多個交易
            of counterparties and customers. However, the Group is                        方及客戶分攤。然而，本集團的
            exposed to the concentration on geographic segment in the                     風險集中於中國之地區分部。於

            Debt instruments at FVTOCI                                                 按公平值計入其他全面收益之債
                                                                                       務工具
            The Group only invests in debt securities issued by investment             本集團只投資於優質投資對手方
            counterparties with high quality. The Directors focus on the               發行的債務證券。董事重視投資
            investments’ credit ratings changes. The Directors assess                  的信貸評級變化。董事根據主
            ECL on the debt instruments at FVTOCI based on the default                 要國際信貸評級機構公佈的適
            rates published by major international credit rating agencies              用於相應債務工具信貸等評級的
            that are applicable to the respective debts instruments credit             違約率，評估按公平值計入其他
            grades. As a result of this assessment, the Directors are of the           全面收益的債務工具的預期信貸
            opinion that the ECL on these debt instruments at FVTOCI is                虧損。基於該評估，董事認為該
            insignificant.                                                             等按公平值計入其他全面收益的
